# Decurix: file-by-file index

Static inventory reviewed on 2026-10-28, including the current working tree's existing uncommitted files. See the [product and architecture guide](codebase-understanding.md) for the connected interpretation.

**Coverage:** 1,203 files, 1,198 text files, five binary assets and 306,094 text lines. Counts exclude this review's generated documents, ignored local secrets, Git internals, dependency directories and build outputs. Local permission values and migration manifest values are omitted.

Each file was read for static indexing. Entries combine source comments, definitions, routes, test names and manually clarified roles for central files. Definitions are representative, not exhaustive. A listed test describes coverage present in source, not a test executed in this review. Comments may reflect older behavior; startup wiring and implementation take precedence. No build or live workflow was run.

| File | Kind | Lines | Role and representative definitions |

| [atomizer.py](../agent/browser-agent/core/atomizer.py) | source | 112 | Live, state-aware atomization: instead of splitting the whole task into atomic steps upfront (blind to the page), this decides ONE atomic, Playwright-sized instruction at a time, given the live page and whatever of the original task is still left -- so it can react to things an upfront plan can't know about (a cookie banner, needing to scroll first, an unexpected redirect) instead of blindly executing a plan made before the page loaded. Definitions: _strip_wrapping, is_done, next_step. |
| [models.py](../agent/browser-agent/core/models.py) | source | 157 | Model abstraction over Ollama's NATIVE /api/chat endpoint (not the OpenAI shim, which silently caps context at ~4096 and ignores num_ctx -- see doc-ui/server.py for the same lesson learned earlier in this workspace). Ollama owns model loading/eviction/VRAM management, so switching models in the UI is just changing which `model` name goes in the request -- no process juggling like the raw llama-server path Phase 1 used. Definitions: resident_model_names, will_swap, chat, chat_stream. |
| [reasoning.py](../agent/browser-agent/core/reasoning.py) | source | 134 | Small LLM calls around the main codegen loop: - expected_outcome / verify_outcome: state up front what should be true after an action succeeds, then check it against the page afterwards, so "the code ran without throwing" isn't mistaken for "the instruction was satisfied." - answer_from_page: grounded Q&A over the current page (used by the manual Interrogate feature) -- one LLM call, no code execution at all. All of these take the page's actual cleaned HTML (see worker.py's g Definitions: expected_outcome, verify_outcome, answer_from_page. |

Reuses Ollama's own GGUF blobs directly -- no re-download. Ollama and this llama-server process both want the whole 16GB card, so only one can be resident at a time: ensure_started() evicts every Ollama-resident model before booting llama-serve Definitions: _health, is_running, stop_if_running, _free_ollama_vram, _wait_for_free_vram, ensure_started … (7 total). |

| [.gitattributes](../agent/thebot/.gitattributes) | source | 19 | Everything here is built and run on Linux (the VM image), so line endings must stay LF in the repo regardless of the checkout platform. Never touch binaries. |

|---|---|---:|---|
| [atomizer.go](../agent/thebot/internal/atomizer/atomizer.go) | source | 431 | Package atomizer decides task shape, in three layers that each cost more than the one before and are only reached when the cheaper one cannot decide: 1. Classify (this file) - deterministic regex classifiers that name the one skill a step belongs to. "Never ask a model to judge its own task granularity" was a hard-won rule in the Node prototype this replaces, and it carries over. 2. Triage (triage.go) - still deterministic: whether a whole request is conversation, one skill's Definitions: Register, Classify, withoutMissingBrowser, stripNonCodeFilenames, isCoding, describesSoftware … (9 total). |
| [atomizer_test.go](../agent/thebot/internal/atomizer/atomizer_test.go) | test | 672 | Atomize is Planner.Atomize without a shared system frame. dropNoOpSteps is steps without the ones noOpStepRe matches (what plan parsing drops). stubProvider stands in for a real model so planning behaviour can be tested deterministically, without a live Ollama or model variance. |

Independent adaptive inference service, specialist routing, resources, downloads, media and model evaluation.
